🧭 Imitation Learning / PolicyImitation Learning / Policy

· 发展脉络· Development

模仿学习与策略方向正经历从单一任务的行为克隆向通用、具身世界模型驱动的范式转变，核心在于解决长视距规划、接触丰富操作及跨具身泛化难题。演进路径从早期的基于轨迹预测的扩散策略，迅速转向融合视觉-语言-动作（VLA）的端到端大模型，并引入力觉、触觉等多模态感知以增强物理交互能力。近期发展聚焦于利用世界模型进行预测与规划，通过一致性蒸馏、动作分块及自反思机制提升推理速度与鲁棒性，同时致力于构建大规模开放数据集与仿真环境以弥合虚实鸿沟。Imitation learning and policy directions are undergoing a paradigm shift from single-task behavior cloning to general, embodied world model-driven approaches, with the core focus on solving long-horizon planning, contact-rich manipulation, and cross-embodiment generalization challenges. The evolutionary path has rapidly shifted from early trajectory-prediction-based diffusion policies to end-to-end large models integrating vision-language-action (VLA), while incorporating force and tactile multi-modal perception to enhance physical interaction capabilities. Recent developments focus on leveraging world models for prediction and planning, improving inference speed and robustness through consistency distillation, action chunking, and self-reflection mechanisms, while also striving to build large-scale open datasets and simulation environments to bridge the sim-to-real gap.

  • 基于视频与人类演示的零/少样本学习Zero/few-shot learning based on video and human demonstrations
  • VLA 架构优化与高效微调VLA Architecture Optimization and Efficient Fine-tuning
  • 扩散模型在在线强化学习中的应用Application of Diffusion Models in Online Reinforcement Learning
  • 全身遥操作与具身接口创新Whole-body teleoperation and embodied interface innovations
  • Imitation Learning / PolicyImitation Learning / Policy

· 各角度时间线· Timeline per Perspective

扩散策略加速与一致性蒸馏Diffusion Policy Acceleration and Consistency Distillation

本方向聚焦于解决扩散策略与流匹配模型在具身智能中推理延迟高的核心痛点。核心思路是通过一致性蒸馏、截断扩散、流匹配优化及几何先验注入等技术，将多步迭代去噪过程压缩为单步或极少步推理。演进路径从早期的直接蒸馏（牺牲多模态换取速度）发展到引入截断锚点、频域优化及显式几何记忆，旨在平衡实时控制需求与复杂任务下的多模态生成能力，推动生成式策略从离线训练走向高频率实时部署。This direction focuses on addressing the core pain point of high inference latency in diffusion policies and flow matching models within embodied intelligence. The core approach involves compressing multi-step iterative denoising processes into single-step or few-step inference through techniques such as consistency distillation, truncated diffusion, flow matching optimization, and geometric prior injection. The evolutionary path has progressed from early direct distillation (trading multimodality for speed) to introducing truncated anchors, frequency-domain optimization, and explicit geometric memory, aiming to balance real-time control requirements with multimodal generation capabilities under complex tasks, thereby promoting generative policies from offline training to high-frequency real-time deployment.

2026-09
Continuous Actions from Discrete Minds: Latent-Aligned Planning for End-to-End Autonomous Driving
动机: Motivation: 旨在解决扩散策略迭代去噪导致的推理延迟问题，同时避免离散化动作空间带来的量化误差。Aims to address the inference latency caused by iterative denoising in diffusion policies while avoiding quantization errors introduced by discretizing the action space.
方法: Method: 提出潜空间对齐规划框架，利用冻结的 VQ-VAE 解码器将离散语义映射至连续动作空间，实现单步潜变量预测。Proposes a latent space alignment planning framework that utilizes a frozen VQ-VAE decoder to map discrete semantics to a continuous action space, enabling single-step latent variable prediction.
推进: Advance: 证明了在特定先验下，连续潜空间预测比离散生成更稳健，成功将多步迭代压缩为单步推理，显著降低延迟并提升任务成功率。Demonstrates that continuous latent space prediction is more robust than discrete generation under specific priors, successfully compressing multi-step iterations into single-step inference, significantly reducing latency and improving task success rates.
2026-09
SV-WAM: An Efficient Surround-View World-Action Model for End-to-End Autonomous Driving
动机: Motivation: 试图在保持全视角感知能力的同时，消除世界模型中视频生成分支带来的高计算开销与推理延迟。Attempts to eliminate the high computational overhead and inference latency introduced by the video generation branch in world models while maintaining full-view perception capabilities.
方法: Method: 采用因果掩码切断动作对视频生成的依赖，将未来视频预测从推理输出降级为训练阶段的监督信号，实现推理时仅输出动作。Employs causal masking to sever the dependency of actions on video generation, downgrading future video prediction from an inference output to a training-stage supervision signal, achieving action-only output during inference.
推进: Advance: 通过工程化剪枝策略，在保留空间感知优势的同时将推理延迟大幅压缩，为实时控制提供了无需生成视频的高效替代方案。Through engineering-based pruning strategies, significantly compresses inference latency while retaining spatial perception advantages, providing an efficient video-generation-free alternative for real-time control.
2026-09
DIDO: Distilling Interaction-Centric Dynamics into One-Step Denoising for World Action Models
动机: Motivation: 解决闭环控制中扩散模型多步去噪导致的动态滞后与推理延迟，试图将多步迭代压缩为单步以平衡实时性与精度。Addressing dynamic lag and inference latency caused by multi-step denoising of diffusion models in closed-loop control, this work attempts to compress multi-step iterations into a single step to balance real-time performance and accuracy.
方法: Method: 设计DIDO蒸馏框架，利用交互中心动态（Interaction-Centric Dynamics）与截断锚点技术，结合DINOv3对齐监督信号，将多步去噪过程蒸馏为单步生成。Designing the DIDO distillation framework, utilizing interaction-centric dynamics and truncated anchor techniques, combined with DINOv3 alignment supervision signals, to distill the multi-step denoising process into single-step generation.
推进: Advance: 揭示了背景结构与交互细节在去噪过程中的时序差异，成功在LIBERO基准上达到99.0% SOTA，证明了以空间换时间的蒸馏策略在实时控制中的有效性。Revealing temporal differences between background structure and interaction details during the denoising process, successfully achieving 99.0% SOTA on the LIBERO benchmark, proving the effectiveness of the space-for-time distillation strategy in real-time control.
2024-05
Consistency Policy: Accelerated Visuomotor Policies via Consistency Distillation
动机: Motivation: 针对Diffusion Policy推理步数过多导致嵌入式设备无法实时控制的瓶颈。Addressing the bottleneck where excessive inference steps in Diffusion Policy prevent real-time control on embedded devices.
方法: Method: 利用一致性蒸馏技术，将多步去噪的扩散策略压缩为单步推理模型。Utilizing consistency distillation techniques to compress multi-step denoising diffusion policies into single-step inference models.
推进: Advance: 实现了十倍速度提升，显著降低推理延迟，但牺牲了部分多模态生成的鲁棒性。Achieved a tenfold speedup, significantly reducing inference latency, but at the cost of some robustness in multimodal generation.
2024-11
DiffusionDrive: Truncated Diffusion Model for End-to-End Autonomous Driving
动机: Motivation: 解决开放世界自动驾驶场景中扩散模型推理慢及模式坍塌问题。Solving the issues of slow diffusion model inference and mode collapse in open-world autonomous driving scenarios.
方法: Method: 提出截断扩散模型，利用聚类锚点替代纯高斯噪声，将采样步数缩减至2步。Proposed a truncated diffusion model that utilizes cluster anchors to replace pure Gaussian noise, reducing sampling steps to just 2.
推进: Advance: 在NAVSIM上实现45 FPS实时规划，证明了少量锚点先验即可维持生成质量。Achieved 45 FPS real-time planning on NAVSIM, demonstrating that a small number of anchor priors can maintain generation quality.
2024-12
FlowPolicy: Enabling Fast and Robust 3D Flow-based Policy via Consistency Flow Matching for Robot Manipulation
动机: Motivation: 针对扩散策略在具身智能中因多步迭代去噪导致的推理延迟高、难以满足实时控制需求的问题，亟需一种能压缩采样步数同时保持生成质量的方法。Addressing the high inference latency and difficulty in meeting real-time control requirements caused by multi-step iterative denoising in diffusion policies for embodied intelligence, there is an urgent need for a method that can compress sampling steps while maintaining generation quality.
方法: Method: 提出FlowPolicy，利用一致性流匹配（Consistency Flow Matching）替代传统扩散采样器，将3D点云条件策略的推理过程压缩至单步或极少步完成。We propose FlowPolicy, which utilizes consistency flow matching to replace traditional diffusion samplers, compressing the inference process of 3D point cloud conditional policies to a single step or very few steps.
推进: Advance: 成功将3D策略推理时间从145ms大幅降低至20ms（约7倍加速），且在保持与DP3相当甚至更高成功率的同时，验证了流匹配在3D几何条件上的鲁棒性优于纯扩散方法。We successfully reduced 3D policy inference time from 145ms to 20ms (approximately 7x acceleration), and while maintaining success rates comparable to or higher than DP3, we verified that flow matching exhibits superior robustness over pure diffusion methods on 3D geometric conditions.
2026-06
DVG-WM: Disentangled Video Generation Enables Efficient Embodied World Model for Robotic Manipulation
动机: Motivation: 解决具身世界模型视频生成速度慢且细节模糊，难以满足实时规划需求。Addressing the slow video generation speed of embodied world models and blurred details, which fail to meet real-time planning requirements.
方法: Method: 提出解耦视频生成框架，先低清预览物理交互再高清修复细节，实现潜空间退化加速。Proposed a decoupled video generation framework that first performs low-definition previews of physical interactions and then refines details in high definition, achieving latent space degradation acceleration.
推进: Advance: 加速3.97倍且任务成功率翻倍，验证了分阶段生成在效率与质量间的平衡。Achieved a 3.97x speedup and doubled task success rates, validating the balance between efficiency and quality in phased generation.
2026-06
Flowing With Purpose: Latent Action Guided Flow Matching Policies For Robotic Manipulation
动机: Motivation: 现有流匹配模型依赖各向同性高斯先验，难以适配碎片化的动作空间。Existing flow matching models rely on isotropic Gaussian priors, making them difficult to adapt to fragmented action spaces.
方法: Method: 利用潜在动作模型（LAM）作为导航员引导流匹配，并引入KL正则化优化向量场。Utilized a Latent Action Model (LAM) as a navigator to guide flow matching and introduced KL regularization to optimize the vector field.
推进: Advance: 构建了更干净的动作向量场，在小模型下实现了对比大VLA的优异性能。Constructed a cleaner action vector field, achieving superior performance compared to large VLA models even with small models.
2026-06
Frequency-Aware Flow Matching for Continuous and Consistent Robotic Action Generation
动机: Motivation: 离散动作块处理多频率数据时梯度冲突导致动作抖动，影响高频控制。Gradient conflicts when discrete action blocks process multi-frequency data cause action jitter, affecting high-frequency control.
方法: Method: 提出频域感知流匹配，在频域进行匹配并余弦基重建，引入速度正则项。Proposed frequency-aware flow matching, performing matching in the frequency domain and reconstructing with cosine bases, introducing a velocity regularization term.
推进: Advance: 有效平滑了动作信号，在真机上验证了连续动作生成的稳定性，但高频脉冲处理仍受限。Effectively smoothed action signals, verifying the stability of continuous action generation on real robots, though handling high-frequency pulses remains limited.
2026-06
Invertible Neural Network Adapter for One-Step Flow Matching in Robot Manipulation
动机: Motivation: 针对流匹配模型多步推理导致的高延迟问题，旨在通过可逆神经网络构建结构化潜空间，实现单步推理下的信息无损动作生成。Aiming to address the high latency caused by multi-step inference in flow matching models, this work seeks to construct a structured latent space using reversible neural networks to achieve lossless action generation under single-step inference.
方法: Method: 设计可逆神经网络适配器，将动作生成过程强行映射至可逆潜空间，结合单步流匹配技术进行推理。Designing a reversible neural network adapter to forcibly map the action generation process into a reversible latent space, combined with single-step flow matching technology for inference.
推进: Advance: 成功将推理延迟降低50%，在仿真与真机实验中验证了单步流匹配在保持动作分布完整性方面的有效性。Successfully reducing inference latency by 50%, validating the effectiveness of single-step flow matching in maintaining action distribution integrity through simulation and real-robot experiments.
2026-07
Training-Free Acceleration for Vision-Language-Action Models with Action Caching and Refinement
动机: Motivation: 流匹配模型推理重复计算导致延迟，需利用环境冗余性加速。Flow matching model inference involves redundant calculations causing latency, requiring the utilization of environmental redundancy for acceleration.
方法: Method: 提出动作缓存与检索机制，将去噪过程转化为查表操作，无需额外训练。Proposed an action cache and retrieval mechanism that transforms the denoising process into a table lookup operation, requiring no additional training.
推进: Advance: 利用历史数据相似性实现推理加速，在任务重复场景下显著降低计算开销。Utilized historical data similarity to achieve inference acceleration, significantly reducing computational overhead in scenarios with repeated tasks.
2026-07
Optimal Transport Q-Learning for Flow Policy Steering and Acceleration
动机: Motivation: 扩散/流策略微调成本高且推理步数多，需寻找低成本的加速微调方案。Diffusion/flow policy fine-tuning is costly and involves many inference steps, necessitating the search for low-cost acceleration fine-tuning solutions.
方法: Method: 利用最优传输Q学习（OTQL）对流策略进行引导与加速，结合神经ODE结构。Utilized Optimal Transport Q-Learning (OTQL) to guide and accelerate flow policies, combined with a Neural ODE structure.
推进: Advance: 仅需少量交互即可大幅减少推理步数并提升成功率，为VLA微调提供新路径。Required only a small amount of interaction to significantly reduce inference steps and improve success rates, providing a new path for VLA fine-tuning.
2026-07
Mixture of Frames Policy: Multi-Frame Action Denoising for Bimanual Mobile Manipulation
动机: Motivation: 针对扩散策略在单帧动作表示下分布学习困难及推理延迟高的问题，旨在通过多帧融合降低动作分布复杂度并提升推理效率。Aiming to address the difficulties in distribution learning and high inference latency of diffusion policies under single-frame action representation, this work seeks to reduce action distribution complexity and improve inference efficiency through multi-frame fusion.
方法: Method: 提出多帧专家融合架构，直接在动作空间利用坐标变换和帧不变性进行多帧动作去噪，替代传统的单帧扩散过程。Proposing a multi-frame expert fusion architecture that performs multi-frame action denoising directly in the action space using coordinate transformations and frame invariance, replacing the traditional single-frame diffusion process.
推进: Advance: 通过显式建模多帧时空依赖，在保持生成质量的同时显著简化了动作分布的学习难度，为高频实时控制提供了新的多模态生成范式。By explicitly modeling multi-frame spatiotemporal dependencies, it significantly simplifies the learning difficulty of action distributions while maintaining generation quality, providing a new multi-modal generation paradigm for high-frequency real-time control.
2026-07
SegDiff: Segmented Trajectory Diffusion for Consistent and Adaptive Robot Manipulation
动机: Motivation: 为了解决长视距规划中扩散策略的误差累积问题，并试图在保持多模态能力的同时优化推理步数。To address the error accumulation problem of diffusion policies in long-horizon planning and attempt to optimize inference steps while maintaining multi-modal capabilities.
方法: Method: 引入轨迹分段机制将长轨迹切分为以关键点为锚点的子段，并利用DDIM inversion技术实现动态时间集成以加速去噪过程。Introducing a trajectory segmentation mechanism to divide long trajectories into sub-segments anchored by key points, and utilizing DDIM inversion technology to achieve dynamic time integration for accelerating the denoising process.
推进: Advance: 通过分段锚点策略有效抑制了长序列规划中的误差传播，展示了在仿真环境下通过少量推理步数实现自适应多模态动作生成的可行性。Effectively suppressing error propagation in long-sequence planning through a segmented anchor strategy, demonstrating the feasibility of adaptive multi-modal action generation with a small number of inference steps in simulation environments.
2026-07
D-SafeMPC: Diffusion-Driven Safe Model Predictive Control with Discrete-Time Control Barrier Functions
动机: Motivation: 针对扩散策略在实时部署中缺乏安全约束且多步去噪导致延迟高的问题，需在加速推理的同时保证控制安全性。Addressing the lack of safety constraints in real-time deployment of diffusion policies and the high latency caused by multi-step denoising, requiring safety assurance in control while accelerating inference.
方法: Method: 将离散时间控制障碍函数（CBF）和Lyapunov函数嵌入扩散反向去噪过程，并结合模型预测控制（MPC）进行硬约束投影以优化单步或短步推理。Embedding discrete-time Control Barrier Functions (CBF) and Lyapunov functions into the diffusion reverse denoising process, combined with Model Predictive Control (MPC) for hard constraint projection to optimize single-step or short-step inference.
推进: Advance: 成功将安全约束直接注入生成式策略的去噪流中，在Sim2Real迁移中实现了高成功率的安全实时控制，验证了安全约束与加速推理并行的有效性。Successfully injecting safety constraints directly into the denoising flow of generative policies, achieving high-success-rate safe real-time control in Sim2Real transfer, validating the effectiveness of parallel safety constraints and accelerated inference.
2026-07
GigaWorld-Policy-0.5: A Faster and Stronger WAM Empowered by AutoResearch
动机: Motivation: 针对扩散策略在具身智能中因多模态专家网络耦合导致的推理延迟高问题，旨在通过架构解耦实现实时控制。Aiming to address the high inference latency caused by the coupling of multi-modal expert networks in embodied intelligence diffusion policies, this work seeks to achieve real-time control through architectural decoupling.
方法: Method: 引入MoT架构将视觉与动作专家分离，训练时利用未来视频预测，推理时仅激活轻量级动作专家网络。Introducing the MoT architecture to separate visual and action experts, utilizing future video prediction during training and activating only a lightweight action expert network during inference.
推进: Advance: 在保持生成能力的同时将推理延迟压缩至85ms，并成功实现C++部署，验证了解耦设计在物理落地任务中的工程有效性。Compressing inference latency to 85ms while maintaining generative capabilities, and successfully deploying in C++, verifying the engineering effectiveness of the decoupled design in physical deployment tasks.
2026-07
Evolving Cache Schedules for Fast Diffusion Policy Inference
动机: Motivation: 为突破扩散策略推理中固定采样步数带来的计算冗余，探索无需蒸馏即可通过调度优化降低算力的新路径。To break through the computational redundancy caused by fixed sampling steps in diffusion policy inference, exploring a new path to reduce computational power requirements through scheduling optimization without distillation.
方法: Method: 提出EVO进化搜索框架，在块-时间格点上全局优化缓存刷新策略，利用冗余感知初始化降低搜索成本。Proposing the EVO evolutionary search framework to globally optimize cache refresh strategies on block-time grids, utilizing redundancy-aware initialization to reduce search costs.
推进: Advance: 实现了无需修改模型权重的训练无关加速，声称在特定场景下可节省8倍算力，为实时部署提供了基于离线优化的新范式。Achieving training-agnostic acceleration without modifying model weights, claiming an 8x reduction in computational power in specific scenarios, providing a new paradigm for real-time deployment based on offline optimization.
2026-07
Diffusion ReRoll: Revisable Denoising for Robotic Sequential Prediction
动机: Motivation: 解决传统扩散策略在长序列预测中早期错误难以修正、导致累积误差和推理失败的核心痛点。Addressing the core pain point of traditional diffusion policies where early errors in long-sequence prediction are difficult to correct, leading to accumulated errors and inference failure.
方法: Method: 设计Diffusion ReRoll机制，在局部序列稳定后主动重新加噪，允许前后段进行双向修正与迭代。Designing the Diffusion ReRoll mechanism to actively re-noise after local sequence stability, allowing bidirectional correction and iteration between preceding and succeeding segments.
推进: Advance: 将扩散过程本身转化为可修订接口，显著提升了Maze和LIBERO等复杂任务中的序列预测成功率，无需修改骨干网络即可实现鲁棒性增强。Transforming the diffusion process itself into a revisable interface, significantly improving sequence prediction success rates in complex tasks like Maze and LIBERO, achieving robustness enhancement without modifying the backbone network.
2026-07
PAC-DP: PAC-Bayesian Diffusion Policy Learning
动机: Motivation: 旨在解决扩散策略在低数据场景下的泛化难题，试图通过理论约束减少对大规模数据的依赖，从而间接降低因数据不足导致的策略不稳定和推理试错成本。Aiming to address the generalization challenges of diffusion policies in low-data scenarios, it attempts to reduce reliance on large-scale data through theoretical constraints, thereby indirectly lowering policy instability and inference trial-and-error costs caused by data scarcity.
方法: Method: 将扩散策略建模为贝叶斯神经网络，引入PAC-Bayes框架与KL正则化项，通过理论界约束策略分布以提升样本效率。Models diffusion policies as Bayesian Neural Networks, introducing the PAC-Bayes framework and KL regularization terms to constrain policy distributions via theoretical bounds, thereby improving sample efficiency.
推进: Advance: 证明了在低数据条件下理论正则化能显著提升复杂任务（如ToolHang）的泛化性能，但揭示了其在数据充足时收益递减且BNN采样增加了推理开销的局限性。Demonstrates that theoretical regularization significantly enhances generalization performance on complex tasks (e.g., ToolHang) under low-data conditions, but reveals limitations including diminishing returns when data is abundant and increased inference overhead due to BNN sampling.
2026-07
FutureRTC: Real-Time Robot Execution with Anticipatory-Conditioned Action Chunking
动机: Motivation: 针对Flow Matching等生成式模型推理延迟导致动作基于过时状态信息的问题，试图在不修改主干网络的前提下，通过异步执行机制实现实时控制。Addresses the issue of action generation based on outdated state information caused by inference latency in generative models like Flow Matching, attempting to achieve real-time control via an asynchronous execution mechanism without modifying the backbone network.
方法: Method: 提出“未来适配器”模块，利用运动先验进行状态修正（State Correction）并预测未来观测（Observation Prediction），以补偿推理延迟带来的信息滞后。Proposes a 'Future Adapter' module that utilizes motion priors for state correction and future observation prediction to compensate for information lag caused by inference latency.

触觉与力控模态融合Fusion of tactile and force control modalities

该方向旨在解决接触丰富任务中纯视觉感知的盲区与力控精度不足问题，核心思路是将力/触觉数据作为一等模态直接融入策略学习。演进脉络从早期的遥操作力信号提取（如DexForce），发展到利用扩散模型生成力域动作（TacDiffusion, DIPCOM），再到近期将力觉深度集成进VLA架构（ForceVLA, TLA）及世界模型（VT-WAM）。最新趋势包括利用低成本传感器（sEMG, 压力垫）反推力信息，以及通过“脑补”触觉弥补硬件缺失，逐步从单一模态融合迈向多模态物理智能的通用化与低成本化。This direction aims to address the blind spots of pure visual perception and insufficient force control precision in contact-rich tasks, with the core strategy of integrating force/tactile data as a first-class modality directly into policy learning. The evolutionary trajectory has progressed from early extraction of force signals in teleoperation (e.g., DexForce), to generating force-domain actions using diffusion models (TacDiffusion, DIPCOM), and recently to deeply integrating force perception into VLA architectures (ForceVLA, TLA) and world models (VT-WAM). Latest trends include inferring force information from low-cost sensors (sEMG, pressure mats) and compensating for hardware deficiencies through 'hallucinated' tactile feedback, gradually shifting from single-modality fusion towards the generalization and cost-reduction of multi-modal physical intelligence.

动机: Motivation: 旨在解决接触丰富任务中依赖昂贵力控硬件的落地瓶颈，试图通过低成本传感器less方案替代传统力传感器以解决VLA模型在接触任务中的感知盲区。Aims to address the deployment bottleneck in contact-rich tasks that relies on expensive force-control hardware, attempting to replace traditional force sensors with a low-cost sensorless solution to overcome the perception blind spots of VLA models in contact tasks.
方法: Method: 提出FWBC-VLA架构，利用双LSTM网络（HSR-Force）从视觉数据中隐式推断力信号，并将估计的力信息直接注入VLA的Action Expert模块进行全身补偿。Proposes the FWBC-VLA architecture, utilizing a dual LSTM network (HSR-Force) to implicitly infer force signals from visual data and directly injecting the estimated force information into the VLA's Action Expert module for whole-body compensation.
推进: Advance: 证明了无需真力传感器即可实现与真力数据相当的控制精度，显著降低了硬件成本，但同时也揭示了模型可能过拟合特定平台动力学特性而缺乏跨平台泛化能力的风险。Demonstrates that control accuracy comparable to true force data can be achieved without true force sensors, significantly reducing hardware costs, but also reveals the risk of the model overfitting to specific platform dynamics and lacking cross-platform generalization capabilities.

动机: Motivation: 针对多模态融合中模型易将无关传感器信号（如背景纹理）与任务强行绑定的“联觉诈骗”问题，解决在有限演示数据下力/触觉模态被噪声干扰导致的策略崩溃。Addresses the 'synesthesia fraud' problem in multimodal fusion where models erroneously bind irrelevant sensor signals (e.g., background textures) to tasks, solving strategy collapse caused by noise interference in force/tactile modalities under limited demonstration data.

推进: Advance: 打破了盲目融合多模态的教条，展示了在特定任务下忽略无用触觉信号能显著提升策略鲁棒性，但也暴露了该方法对预定义规则依赖过强、难以应对无明确信号定义复杂任务的局限。Breaks the dogma of blind multimodal fusion, demonstrating that ignoring useless tactile signals in specific tasks can significantly improve strategy robustness, but also exposes the limitation of the method's strong dependence on predefined rules and difficulty in handling complex tasks without clear signal definitions.

推进: Advance: 实验证明对齐数据接口与动作空间可将任务成功率从40%提升至76.8%，并证实50Hz低频推理即可实现稳定力控，打破了力控必须高频的固有认知。Experiments prove that aligning data interfaces with action spaces can increase task success rates from 40% to 76.8%, and confirm that 50Hz low-frequency inference can achieve stable force control, breaking the inherent cognition that force control must be high-frequency.

基于视频与人类演示的零/少样本学习Zero/few-shot learning based on video and human demonstrations

该方向旨在突破机器人真机数据稀缺瓶颈，利用互联网视频、遥操作及人类演示实现零/少样本策略学习。核心思路从直接模仿动作转向学习中间表示（如点轨迹、光流、潜动作）或结果约束，通过数据编辑、跨域对齐及生成式仿真技术，将非结构化人类视频转化为机器人可执行的策略。演进路径从早期的遥操作数据增强，发展到利用大模型进行视频生成与编辑（如Phantom），再到构建包含人类交互的社会化数据集（HABIT）及几何感知的潜变量学习，逐步实现从“人类动作映射”到“视觉结果对齐”的范式转变，最终追求在无需真机微调下的零样本泛化能力。This direction aims to break the bottleneck of scarce real-robot data by leveraging internet videos, teleoperation, and human demonstrations to achieve zero/few-shot policy learning. The core approach shifts from direct action imitation to learning intermediate representations (e.g., point trajectories, optical flow, latent actions) or outcome constraints. Through data editing, cross-domain alignment, and generative simulation techniques, unstructured human videos are transformed into executable robot policies. The evolutionary path progresses from early teleoperation data augmentation to utilizing large models for video generation and editing (e.g., Phantom), and further to constructing social datasets with human interaction (HABIT) and geometry-aware latent variable learning, gradually realizing a paradigm shift from 'human action mapping' to 'visual outcome alignment', ultimately pursuing zero-shot generalization without real-robot fine-tuning.

GIFT: Guided Intermediate Feature Training via Action-Oriented Structural Supervision for Robotic Manipulation
动机: Motivation: 针对视觉语言动作模型（VLA）与机器人本体（WAM）之间存在动作充分性缺口的问题，旨在解决仅靠视频模仿难以直接生成有效动作策略的难题。Addresses the action sufficiency gap between Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models and the robot body (WAM), aiming to solve the difficulty of directly generating effective action strategies relying solely on video imitation.
方法: Method: 提出特征蒸馏框架，通过引入几何、功能（Affordance）和目标的辅助监督信号，对中间特征进行结构化约束，实现架构无关的特征内部化。Proposes a feature distillation framework that introduces auxiliary supervision signals for geometry, affordance, and goals to structurally constrain intermediate features, achieving architecture-agnostic feature internalization.
推进: Advance: 证明了特征内部化优于外挂条件注入，显著提升了零样本迁移能力，并在 LIBERO-Plus 上验证了跨策略的泛化效果。Demonstrates that feature internalization outperforms external condition injection, significantly improving zero-shot transfer capabilities, and validates cross-strategy generalization effects on LIBERO-Plus.

方法: Method: 构建世界锚点动作模型，通过预测潜在空间中的语义动态对齐来替代像素级视频生成，利用锚定机制过滤无关视觉信息。Constructs a World Anchor Action Model that replaces pixel-level video generation by aligning semantic dynamics in latent space, utilizing an anchoring mechanism to filter out irrelevant visual information.
推进: Advance: 证明了跳过像素生成直接学习语义动态可大幅降低推理成本并提升泛化能力，在零样本场景下有效解决了视觉干扰导致的策略失效问题。Demonstrates that learning semantic dynamics directly while skipping pixel generation can significantly reduce inference costs and improve generalization, effectively solving policy failure caused by visual interference in zero-shot scenarios.

Practice Makes Policies: Bootstrapping and Consolidating Robotic Capabilities from Zero Human Demonstrations
动机: Motivation: 旨在解决从零人类示范起步时策略冷启动困难及真机数据稀缺的问题，试图构建从推理到肌肉记忆的自主演化路径。Aiming to solve the difficulties of policy cold-start when starting from zero human demonstrations and the scarcity of real-robot data, it attempts to construct an autonomous evolutionary path from reasoning to muscle memory.
方法: Method: 提出分层架构，利用VLM进行零样本猜测、复用旧经验及训练策略，并通过调度器协调不同层级的能力以执行任务。Proposing a hierarchical architecture, it utilizes VLMs for zero-shot guessing, reusing past experiences, and training policies, while employing a scheduler to coordinate capabilities across different levels to execute tasks.
推进: Advance: 实现了从纯推理到闭环策略的演化，在开放场景下达到86%成功率，为具身智能的“肌肉记忆”理论提供了实证，但推理延迟仍是瓶颈。Achieving an evolution from pure reasoning to closed-loop policies, it reaches an 86% success rate in open scenarios, providing empirical evidence for the "muscle memory" theory in embodied intelligence, though inference latency remains a bottleneck.

ShadowDancer: Teaching Video World Models Any Action by Learning Unified Dynamics Representations from a Video and Its Shadow
动机: Motivation: 解决现有潜在动作模型在视频生成中难以解耦“动作语义”与“外观纹理”的问题，防止模型过拟合背景导致跨域迁移时出现鬼影或动作失真。Addressing the difficulty of decoupling 'action semantics' from 'appearance texture' in video generation by existing latent action models, preventing model overfitting to backgrounds that leads to ghosting or motion distortion during cross-domain transfer.
方法: Method: 构建“影子库”（Shadow Library），利用渲染引擎生成动作与外观解耦的成对数据，强制模型仅学习动作动力学而非视觉表象。Constructing a 'Shadow Library' that uses rendering engines to generate paired data with decoupled actions and appearances, forcing the model to learn only action dynamics rather than visual appearances.
推进: Advance: 通过引入因果解耦机制，显著提升了视频世界模型在动作迁移任务中的泛化能力，证明了在仿真中构建纯净动作表征是通向真实世界策略的关键路径。Significantly improving the generalization ability of video world models in action transfer tasks by introducing a causal decoupling mechanism, proving that constructing pure action representations in simulation is a key path to real-world policies.

推进: Advance: 验证了带有强几何约束的合成数据能有效弥补真实演示数据的不足，为少样本学习提供了低成本、高一致性的替代方案。Validating that synthetic data with strong geometric constraints can effectively compensate for the lack of real demonstration data, providing a low-cost, high-consistency alternative for few-shot learning.

方法: Method: 设计Touch2Robot闭环系统，在演示阶段向人类提供机器人触觉的视觉化反馈，并引入强化学习蒸馏机制优化触觉重建模型。Designing the Touch2Robot closed-loop system, providing visualized feedback of robot tactile sensations to humans during the demonstration phase and introducing a reinforcement learning distillation mechanism to optimize the tactile reconstruction model.
推进: Advance: 通过引入触觉感知回路，使回放成功率翻倍，验证了“让演示者看见机器人触觉”能显著缩小形态鸿沟并提升策略质量。Doubling the replay success rate by introducing a tactile perception loop, verifying that 'letting demonstrators see robot tactile feedback' significantly narrows the morphology gap and improves policy quality.

Zeva-Ego: Egocentric Mid-Training with In-Context Causal Learning for Robot Manipulation
动机: Motivation: 面对海量人类视频数据中动作几何信息难以直接迁移、且机器人本体差异导致策略泛化性差的挑战。Facing the challenges of difficult direct transfer of action geometric information in massive human video data and poor strategy generalization due to robot embodiment differences.
方法: Method: 提出Zeva-Ego框架，利用因果记忆库（ICCL）在训练过程中进行上下文因果学习，从人类视频中提取解耦的动作几何特征。Proposed the Zeva-Ego framework, utilizing a Causal Memory Library (ICCL) for context-aware causal learning during training to extract decoupled action geometric features from human videos.
推进: Advance: 实现了无需真机微调的零样本迁移，证明了从非结构化视频中提取物理常识并冻结参数进行推理的可行性。Achieved zero-shot transfer without real-robot fine-tuning, demonstrating the feasibility of extracting physical commonsense from unstructured videos and performing inference with frozen parameters.

Counterfactual Video Generation Enables Scalable Humanoid Loco-Manipulation
动机: Motivation: 针对人形机器人具身交互数据稀缺及真机采集成本高昂的瓶颈，旨在利用少量真实视频通过生成式模型构建大规模反事实交互数据以训练策略。Aims to utilize generative models to construct large-scale counterfactual interaction data from a small amount of real video for training policies, addressing the bottleneck of scarce embodied interaction data for humanoid robots and high costs of real-robot collection.
方法: Method: 提出PRISM框架，利用视频生成模型从少量种子视频中合成反事实的 locomotion-manipulation 视频，并将生成的数据转化为仿真环境中的物理合理轨迹进行训练。Proposes the PRISM framework, using video generation models to synthesize counterfactual locomotion-manipulation videos from a few seed videos and converting the generated data into physically reasonable trajectories in simulation environments for training.

EgoAlign: Bridging the Human-Humanoid Gap for Long-Range Loco-Manipulation
动机: Motivation: 为了解决人类视频与人形机器人身体结构差异导致的动作映射困难，试图在无需真机遥操作的情况下低成本获取长距离搬运技能。Attempts to low-costly acquire long-distance carrying skills without real-robot teleoperation, addressing the difficulty in action mapping caused by the structural differences between human videos and humanoid robot bodies.
方法: Method: 构建EgoAlign框架，利用仿真器MuJoCo和特定控制器SONIC将人类第一视角视频动作编码为机器人可执行的token序列，实现端到端的动作翻译。Constructs the EgoAlign framework, utilizing the MuJoCo simulator and the specific SONIC controller to encode human first-person video actions into robot-executable token sequences, achieving end-to-end action translation.
推进: Advance: 显著提升了人类经验向机器人身体移植的效率（比遥操作快5倍），成功在真机上实现了10米长距离搬运的零样本执行，验证了跨域动作对齐的可行性。Significantly improves the efficiency of transplanting human experience to robot bodies (5x faster than teleoperation), successfully achieving zero-shot execution of 10-meter long-distance carrying on real robots, validating the feasibility of cross-domain action alignment.

VLA 架构优化与高效微调VLA Architecture Optimization and Efficient Fine-tuning

本方向聚焦于视觉 - 语言 - 动作（VLA）模型的轻量化架构与高效微调，旨在解决大模型在具身智能中部署难、泛化差及推理慢的痛点。核心思路从单纯堆砌参数转向“数据效率”与“推理机制”的双重优化：一方面通过隐式动作、任务中心表征及知识蒸馏压缩模型体积（如SmolVLA, UniVLA）；另一方面引入视觉思维链、物理约束及历史状态显式建模（如FlowVLA, Chronos）以增强空间感知与长程规划能力。演进路径呈现出从“通用模仿”向“物理可解释”、“动态适应”及“低成本跨实体迁移”的务实转变，强调在有限算力下通过架构创新与数据策略提升真实场景的鲁棒性。This direction focuses on lightweight architectures and efficient fine-tuning of Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models, aiming to address the pain points of difficult deployment, poor generalization, and slow inference of large models in embodied intelligence. The core approach shifts from simply stacking parameters to dual optimization of 'data efficiency' and 'inference mechanisms': on one hand, compressing model volume through implicit actions, task-centric representations, and knowledge distillation (e.g., SmolVLA, UniVLA); on the other hand, introducing visual chain-of-thought, physical constraints, and explicit modeling of historical states (e.g., FlowVLA, Chronos) to enhance spatial perception and long-horizon planning capabilities. The evolutionary path shows a pragmatic shift from 'general imitation' to 'physically interpretable', 'dynamic adaptation', and 'low-cost cross-entity transfer', emphasizing robustness in real-world scenarios through architectural innovation and data strategies under limited computing power.

动机: Motivation: 为缓解连续环境中强化学习面临的稀疏奖励与长序列Credit Assignment难题，试图通过宏观动作抽象降低决策复杂度。Attempts to reduce decision complexity by abstracting macro-actions to address the sparse reward and long-sequence credit assignment challenges faced by reinforcement learning in continuous environments.

动机: Motivation: 分层 VLA 模型常面临动作块长度固定或停止信号难以预测的接口问题，导致长程任务执行效率低下且耦合度高。Hierarchical VLA models often face interface issues with fixed action block lengths or unpredictable stop signals, leading to low execution efficiency and high coupling in long-horizon tasks.
方法: Method: 提出 SparkVLA，设计停止感知机制与自适应动作块切分，利用离线序数偏好训练统一评估“停止”与“长度”的决策。We propose SparkVLA, designing a stop-aware mechanism and adaptive action block splitting, utilizing offline ordinal preference training to unify the decision-making for 'stop' and 'length'.
推进: Advance: 解决了长程任务中动作块接口的耦合难题，在 RoboCerebra 基准上显著超越基线，展示了自适应块切分对提升长程规划灵活性的价值。We solved the coupling problem of action block interfaces in long-horizon tasks, significantly outperforming baselines on the RoboCerebra benchmark, demonstrating the value of adaptive block splitting in enhancing long-horizon planning flexibility.

推进: Advance: 证明了在垂直医疗领域引入特定模态（超声）作为 VLA 输入能显著提升任务理解与执行精度，为专用场景的高效微调提供了新范式。We demonstrated that introducing specific modalities (ultrasound) as VLA inputs in vertical medical fields significantly improves task understanding and execution accuracy, providing a new paradigm for efficient fine-tuning in dedicated scenarios.

推进: Advance: 展示了通过架构解耦与海量数据堆料可激发涌现能力，验证了模块化设计在提升具身智能模型物理可解释性与跨场景泛化上的潜力。We demonstrated that architectural decoupling and massive data scaling can trigger emergent capabilities, verifying the potential of modular design in enhancing the physical interpretability and cross-scenario generalization of embodied intelligence models.

方法: Method: 提出基于角色条件的子令牌路由机制，在保留Token位置信息的同时动态压缩值向量维度，将KV缓存压缩至原大小的9%-11%。Proposing a role-conditioned sub-token routing mechanism that dynamically compresses value vector dimensions while preserving token position information, compressing the KV cache to 9%-11% of its original size.
推进: Advance: 相比传统剪枝，该“精修”策略在LIBERO长视界任务中实现了更优的压缩率与性能平衡，揭示了跨模态压缩的耦合效应。Compared to traditional pruning, this 'refinement' strategy achieves a better balance between compression rate and performance in LIBERO long-horizon tasks, revealing the coupling effects of cross-modal compression.

推进: Advance: 打破了TTE优化的黑盒状态，明确了在算力冗余场景下榨干冻结策略潜力的具体边界，为低成本提升泛化性提供了新范式。Breaking the black-box state of TTE optimization, clarifying the specific boundaries for extracting the full potential of frozen strategies in scenarios with computational redundancy, providing a new paradigm for low-cost generalization improvement.

动机: Motivation: 为了解决行为克隆中“来者不拒”导致的泛化差及在线推理计算冗余问题，试图在训练与部署间解耦预测与执行。To address the poor generalization and computational redundancy in online inference caused by the "accept all" nature of behavior cloning, we attempt to decouple prediction from execution between training and deployment.
方法: Method: 提出PAVE框架，训练阶段通过预测未来状态和价值评分进行自我进化，部署阶段仅保留精简的正反馈执行器，剔除复杂的预测模块。We propose the PAVE framework, where the training phase involves self-evolution through predicting future states and value scores, while the deployment phase retains only a streamlined positive feedback actuator, discarding complex prediction modules.
推进: Advance: 实现了训练时的高精度感知与推理时的极致效率平衡，验证了“离线卷生卷死，上线躺平输出”的架构优化策略在资源受限场景的可行性。We achieve a balance between high-precision perception during training and extreme efficiency during inference, validating the feasibility of the "offline intensive training, online relaxed output" architecture optimization strategy in resource-constrained scenarios.

推进: Advance: 在LIBERO及真机实验中实现了10%-20%的性能提升，证实了基于语义技能检索的适配策略比全量重训更具数据效率。We achieve a 10%-20% performance improvement in LIBERO and real-robot experiments, confirming that adaptation strategies based on semantic skill retrieval are more data-efficient than full retraining.

动机: Motivation: 针对VLA模型在长动作序列预测中因注意力分散导致的“胡言乱语”及翻车风险，旨在通过内部机制实现动态的推理控制。Addressing the "nonsense" and crash risks caused by attention dispersion in long action sequence prediction by VLA models, this work aims to achieve dynamic inference control through internal mechanisms.
方法: Method: 利用内部Cross-Attention的熵值变化作为监控信号，动态截断动作块（Action Chunking），在模型置信度下降时自动停止生成。We utilize the entropy variation of internal Cross-Attention as a monitoring signal to dynamically truncate action chunks, automatically stopping generation when model confidence drops.
推进: Advance: 实现了零额外训练开销的动态止损机制，显著提升了高频控制下的安全性，证明了利用架构内部动态特征优化推理效率的潜力。We implement a dynamic stop-loss mechanism with zero additional training overhead, significantly enhancing safety under high-frequency control and demonstrating the potential of optimizing inference efficiency using internal architectural dynamic features.

动机: Motivation: 针对现有VLA模型在Flow Matching中忽略动作信号频谱异质性（低频趋势与高频微调混同）导致推理效率低下的问题。Addressing the low inference efficiency caused by existing VLA models ignoring the spectral heterogeneity of action signals (mixing low-frequency trends with high-frequency fine-tuning) in Flow Matching.

RoboDrop: Curating VLA Post-Training Data via Local Gradient Compatibility
动机: Motivation: 为了解决VLA后训练数据中低质量样本导致策略收敛困难，且传统基于轨迹长度或相似度的清洗方法不够精细的问题。To address the difficulty in policy convergence caused by low-quality samples in VLA post-training data, and the lack of granularity in traditional cleaning methods based on trajectory length or similarity.
方法: Method: 提出RoboDrop方法，通过计算样本梯度与干净验证集梯度的局部兼容性来筛选数据，替代单纯的特征相似度匹配。Proposing the RoboDrop method, it screens data by calculating the local compatibility between sample gradients and gradients from a clean validation set, replacing simple feature similarity matching.
推进: Advance: 证明了梯度方向比特征相似更能反映监督质量，在真实机器人任务中将成功率从35%大幅提升至67.5%，实现了数据效率的质变。Proving that gradient direction reflects supervision quality better than feature similarity, it significantly increases success rates from 35% to 67.5% in real robot tasks, achieving a qualitative leap in data efficiency.

Time-Frequency Geometric Cross-Attention for Chunked Vision-Language-Action Models
动机: Motivation: 针对标准点积注意力机制无法有效捕捉动作Chunk中不同阶段（如抓取与调整）在向量空间正交关系的缺陷。Addressing the defect that standard dot-product attention mechanisms cannot effectively capture the orthogonal relationships in vector space between different stages (e.g., grasping and adjustment) within action chunks.
方法: Method: 设计时频几何交叉注意力（TFGCA）模块，利用小波变换分解动作并引入楔积运算，显式建模动作序列中的正交几何关系。Designing the Time-Frequency Geometric Cross-Attention (TFGCA) module, it decomposes actions using wavelet transforms and introduces wedge product operations to explicitly model orthogonal geometric relationships in action sequences.
推进: Advance: 在分布外（OOD）及真实机器人场景中显著增强了模型的鲁棒性与空间感知能力，验证了几何代数结构在VLA架构中的有效性。Significantly enhancing model robustness and spatial perception capabilities in out-of-distribution (OOD) and real robot scenarios, validating the effectiveness of geometric algebra structures in VLA architectures.

动机: Motivation: 为解决多阶段操作任务中动作Chunk跨越阶段导致标签信息缺失，以及传统蒸馏无法利用未来阶段语义信息的问题。To address the missing label information caused by action chunks spanning stages in multi-stage operation tasks, and the inability of traditional distillation to utilize future stage semantic information.
方法: Method: 提出块对齐语义蒸馏（CASD），将动作Chunk内不同阶段的占比计算为加权混合目标，利用离线VLM注入未来阶段语义。Proposing Chunk-Aligned Semantic Distillation (CASD), it calculates the proportion of different stages within an action chunk as a weighted mixed objective and injects future stage semantics using offline VLMs.
推进: Advance: 成功将“未来知识”融入训练目标，提升了基线模型在多阶段任务中的表现，但也暴露了该方法对离线标注质量及基线模型架构的强依赖性。Successfully integrating "future knowledge" into training objectives, it improves baseline model performance in multi-stage tasks but also exposes strong dependencies on offline annotation quality and baseline model architecture.

推进: Advance: 实现了从静态预训练到动态适应的范式转变，有效缓解了分布偏移问题，但揭示了测试时微调带来的推理延迟与算力成本之间的权衡挑战。Achieving a paradigm shift from static pre-training to dynamic adaptation, it effectively mitigates distribution shift problems but reveals the trade-off challenges between inference latency and computational costs brought by test-time fine-tuning.

动机: Motivation: 旨在解决具身智能中额外训练奖励模型带来的高昂适配成本与算力浪费，试图通过复用VLA策略自身的视觉编码器实现低成本自我评估。Aiming to solve the high adaptation costs and computational waste caused by training additional reward models in embodied intelligence, attempting to achieve low-cost self-evaluation by reusing the visual encoder of the VLA policy itself.
方法: Method: 提出内在机器人奖励（IRR）框架，直接复用VLA策略的视觉骨干网络来评估自身表现，构建无需独立奖励模型的闭环系统。Proposing the Intrinsic Robot Reward (IRR) framework, directly reusing the visual backbone network of the VLA policy to evaluate its own performance, constructing a closed-loop system without independent reward models.
推进: Advance: 验证了在不引入额外感知骨干的前提下，利用策略自身表征进行自主评估的可行性，为低资源下的自进化学习提供了可验证的硬件基础。Validating the feasibility of autonomous evaluation using the policy's own representations without introducing additional perceptual backbones, providing a verifiable hardware foundation for self-evolution learning under low-resource conditions.

动机: Motivation: 针对纯端到端VLA模型在空间深度感知上的先天缺陷，以及工业场景中RGB与LiDAR数据融合困难的问题，寻求轻量化且空间落地的解决方案。Addressing the innate defects of pure end-to-end VLA models in spatial depth perception and the difficulty of fusing RGB and LiDAR data in industrial scenarios, seeking lightweight and spatially deployable solutions.

Taming VLAs under Robot Execution Errors: Self-Compensation and Stress Testing
动机: Motivation: 解决VLA模型在真实物理环境中因传感器噪声、摩擦及背隙等执行误差导致部署失败，且传统离线训练难以覆盖复杂物理动态的问题。Solves the problem of deployment failure in VLA models in real physical environments due to execution errors like sensor noise, friction, and backlash, where traditional offline training struggles to cover complex physical dynamics.
方法: Method: 利用本体感知（proprioception）残差构建在线微调机制，通过LoRA动态更新策略补偿执行误差，并引入RoboStress基准测试对物理噪声进行显式建模。Utilizes proprioception residuals to build an online fine-tuning mechanism, dynamically updating policies via LoRA to compensate for execution errors, and introduces the RoboStress benchmark to explicitly model physical noise.
推进: Advance: 验证了无需复杂强化学习即可通过轻量级在线微调提升30%的鲁棒性，确立了基于残差补偿的务实部署方案，弥补了现有模型在物理噪声下的泛化短板。Validates that lightweight online fine-tuning can improve robustness by 30% without complex reinforcement learning, establishing a pragmatic deployment scheme based on residual compensation to fill the generalization gap of existing models under physical noise.

扩散模型在在线强化学习中的应用Application of Diffusion Models in Online Reinforcement Learning

本方向聚焦于将扩散模型引入在线强化学习（Online RL），旨在解决高斯策略在多模态动作分布建模上的局限性，并应对高维动作空间下的探索难题。核心思路是利用扩散过程的迭代去噪特性作为策略生成器，结合Q函数或熵正则化项，在在线交互中直接优化策略分布。演进脉络从早期的理论构建（如Q-weighted VLO）起步，逐步转向解决推理延迟与熵估计难题（如DACER、DIME），进而发展出无需显式目标分布采样的重加权评分匹配（RSM）等高效算法。尽管在仿真环境（MuJoCo, Humanoid）中显著提升了性能与样本效率，但当前研究仍面临推理速度慢、对Q函数质量依赖强以及真机部署延迟等工程挑战，正从单纯的策略生成向更高效的在线训练框架演进。This direction focuses on introducing diffusion models into online reinforcement learning (Online RL), aiming to address the limitations of Gaussian policies in modeling multi-modal action distributions and the exploration challenges under high-dimensional action spaces. The core idea is to utilize the iterative denoising characteristics of the diffusion process as a policy generator, combined with Q-functions or entropy regularization terms, to directly optimize the policy distribution during online interaction. The evolutionary trajectory began with early theoretical constructions (e.g., Q-weighted VLO), gradually shifted towards solving inference latency and entropy estimation challenges (e.g., DACER, DIME), and further developed efficient algorithms like Reweighted Score Matching (RSM) that do not require explicit target distribution sampling. Although significantly improving performance and sample efficiency in simulation environments (MuJoCo, Humanoid), current research still faces engineering challenges such as slow inference speed, strong dependence on Q-function quality, and deployment latency on real robots, evolving from simple policy generation towards more efficient online training frameworks.

推进: Advance: 成功在复杂地形实现89.7%的成功率，展现出比单纯避障更优的预判加速与物理本质理解能力，验证了扩散模型在具身智能长程规划中的有效性。Successfully achieves an 89.7% success rate in complex terrains, demonstrating superior predictive acceleration and understanding of physical essentials compared to simple obstacle avoidance, validating the effectiveness of diffusion models in long-horizon planning for embodied intelligence.

DIDO: Distilling Interaction-Centric Dynamics into One-Step Denoising for World Action Models
动机: Motivation: 为解决扩散模型在在线闭环控制中因多步去噪迭代引发的推理延迟与动态滞后问题，试图压缩生成过程以提升实时性。To address inference latency and dynamic lag caused by multi-step denoising iterations in diffusion models for online closed-loop control, we attempt to compress the generation process to enhance real-time performance.
方法: Method: 提出DIDO方法，通过蒸馏技术将多步去噪过程压缩为单步生成，并引入交互中心Token与DINOv3对齐信号弥补信息缺失。We propose the DIDO method, which compresses the multi-step denoising process into single-step generation via distillation, and introduces interaction center Tokens and DINOv3 alignment signals to compensate for information loss.

推进: Advance: 实现了在保持高覆盖率的同时显著降低智能体行程差异，证明了条件化扩散策略在生成行为多样性方面的有效性，但揭示了重排序在大规模集群中的计算瓶颈。This approach achieves significant reduction in agent travel variance while maintaining high coverage, demonstrating the effectiveness of conditioned diffusion policies in generating behavioral diversity, yet revealing computational bottlenecks of reordering in large-scale swarms.

动机: Motivation: 挑战模仿学习中长开环执行（Action Chunking）的必要性，指出其本质是策略对非马尔可夫环境适应的妥协，旨在探索更高效的在线反应式策略。Challenging the necessity of long open-loop execution (Action Chunking) in imitation learning, we identify it as a compromise for policy adaptation to non-Markovian environments, aiming to explore more efficient online reactive strategies.

全身遥操作与具身接口创新Whole-body teleoperation and embodied interface innovations

本角度聚焦于通过创新全身遥操作硬件与具身接口，解决人形机器人模仿学习中“数据获取难”与“全身协调差”的痛点。演进脉络从依赖昂贵动捕设备（如TWIST）转向低成本视觉/VR方案（HumanPlus, Bunny-VisionPro），并进一步探索外骨骼（DexUMI）与生物信号（ForceBand）以消除人机物理差距。核心思路在于构建高保真、低延迟的“人-机”映射通道，将人类全身运动与力觉信息高效迁移至机器人。未来趋势正从单一动作模仿向结合扩散模型、VLA大模型的长视界任务规划演进，力求在降低硬件门槛的同时，实现从短程操作到复杂物理交互的通用能力跨越。This perspective focuses on addressing the pain points of 'difficult data acquisition' and 'poor whole-body coordination' in humanoid robot imitation learning through innovations in whole-body teleoperation hardware and embodied interfaces. The evolutionary trajectory shifts from relying on expensive motion capture equipment (e.g., TWIST) to low-cost visual/VR solutions (HumanPlus, Bunny-VisionPro), and further explores exoskeletons (DexUMI) and bio-signals (ForceBand) to eliminate the physical gap between humans and machines. The core idea lies in constructing a high-fidelity, low-latency 'human-machine' mapping channel to efficiently transfer human whole-body motion and force information to robots. Future trends are evolving from single-action imitation to long-horizon task planning combining diffusion models and VLA large models, striving to achieve a general capability leap from short-range manipulation to complex physical interactions while lowering hardware barriers.

Reinforcement Learning with Action Chunking
动机: Motivation: 解决离线强化学习中探索效率低与动作时序连贯性差的问题，提升长视界任务的学习速度。Addresses the low exploration efficiency and poor action temporal coherence in offline reinforcement learning, improving the learning speed for long-horizon tasks.

推进: Advance: 显著提升了长视界稀疏奖励任务下的探索效率与训练稳定性，但计算开销与超参敏感性仍是瓶颈。Significantly improves exploration efficiency and training stability under long-horizon sparse reward tasks, but computational overhead and hyperparameter sensitivity remain bottlenecks.

Imitation Learning / PolicyImitation Learning / Policy

Imitation Learning/Policy 方向正从单一任务的行为克隆向通用、高效、可解释的扩散策略演进。早期工作聚焦于解决高维动作空间的表示与采样效率，如 VQ-BeT 和 BAKU 通过离散化或模块化设计提升推理速度与多任务适应性。随后，3D 视觉与扩散模型的结合（如 3D Diffuser Actor, DP3）显著增强了策略对空间结构的感知与鲁棒性，解决了传统方法对几何信息利用不足的问题。当前趋势转向基础模型与规划能力的融合，如 RDT-1B 和 BeyondMimic 利用大规模预训练与潜空间引导，试图在双臂操作与人形控制中实现零样本泛化与技能组合。同时，理论界（如 Is Behavior Cloning All You Need?）开始重新审视 BC 的样本复杂度边界，而自适应微调技术（如 Steering Your Diffusion Policy）则致力于降低大模型在真实机器人上的部署门槛，推动从“模仿动作”向“理解物理与意图”的跨越。The Imitation Learning/Policy direction is evolving from single-task behavior cloning towards general, efficient, and interpretable diffusion policies. Early work focused on addressing representation and sampling efficiency in high-dimensional action spaces; for instance, VQ-BeT and BAKU improved inference speed and multi-task adaptability through discretization or modular design. Subsequently, the integration of 3D vision with diffusion models (e.g., 3D Diffuser Actor, DP3) significantly enhanced policies' perception and robustness regarding spatial structures, solving the issue of insufficient utilization of geometric information in traditional methods. Current trends shift towards the fusion of foundation models and planning capabilities; for example, RDT-1B and BeyondMimic leverage large-scale pre-training and latent space guidance to achieve zero-shot generalization and skill composition in dual-arm manipulation and humanoid control. Meanwhile, the theoretical community (e.g., Is Behavior Cloning All You Need?) is re-examining the sample complexity boundaries of BC, while adaptive fine-tuning techniques (e.g., Steering Your Diffusion Policy) aim to lower the deployment threshold for large models on real robots, promoting a leap from "imitating actions" to "understanding physics and intent."

Spatially Aware World Action Model via Geometric Latent Diffusion
动机: Motivation: 解决现有世界动作模型（WAM）缺乏深度感知能力，导致在 3D 空间中操作精度受限的问题。Address the limitation of operation accuracy in 3D space caused by the lack of depth perception capabilities in existing World Action Models (WAM).
方法: Method: 提出空间感知 WAM（SA-WAM），通过非线性归一化将深度图强行注入冻结的 VAE，利用几何潜扩散进行预测。Propose Spatial-Aware WAM (SA-WAM), which forcibly injects depth maps into a frozen VAE via non-linear normalization and utilizes geometric latent diffusion for prediction.
推进: Advance: 在不微调 VAE 的前提下显著提升了 RoboCasa 等基准的 3D 操作精度，验证了低成本增强空间感知的有效性。Significantly improve 3D operation accuracy on benchmarks like RoboCasa without fine-tuning the VAE, validating the effectiveness of low-cost enhancements for spatial perception.
2026-09
StereoPatch: Patch-Aligned RGB-Depth Fusion for Spatial Perception in Robot Manipulation
动机: Motivation: 针对纯RGB视觉模仿学习中因忽略几何信息导致“脸盲”及接触控制失效的问题，旨在不修改策略架构的前提下增强空间感知能力。Addressing the 'face blindness' and contact control failure in pure RGB visual imitation learning caused by ignoring geometric information, we aim to enhance spatial perception capabilities without modifying the policy architecture.
方法: Method: 提出StereoPatch方法，将深度特征作为补丁强行嵌入RGB Patch中，利用交叉注意力机制实现多模态特征的对齐与融合。We propose the StereoPatch method, which forcibly embeds depth features as patches into RGB Patches, utilizing a cross-attention mechanism to achieve alignment and fusion of multi-modal features.
推进: Advance: 在不改变策略架构的情况下显著提升了泛化性与接触控制精度，证明了轻量级深度融合方案在解决几何感知缺陷上的有效性。We significantly improve generalization and contact control accuracy without altering the policy architecture, demonstrating the effectiveness of lightweight deep fusion solutions in addressing geometric perception defects.
2024-02
3D Diffuser Actor: Policy Diffusion with 3D Scene Representations
动机: Motivation: 为了解决传统扩散策略缺乏显式 3D 空间理解能力，导致在复杂场景下泛化性不足的问题。To address the insufficient generalization of traditional diffusion policies in complex scenes due to the lack of explicit 3D spatial understanding capabilities.
方法: Method: 提出 3D Diffuser Actor，将 3D 场景 Token 化并嵌入 Transformer，利用去噪扩散过程直接生成基于 3D 表示的策略。Proposed 3D Diffuser Actor, which tokenizes 3D scenes and embeds them into a Transformer, utilizing a denoising diffusion process to directly generate policies based on 3D representations.
推进: Advance: 在 RLBench 和 CALVIN 上显著超越现有方法，证明了显式 3D 空间结构建模能大幅提升策略对几何信息的利用效率与鲁棒性。Significantly outperformed existing methods on RLBench and CALVIN, demonstrating that explicit 3D spatial structure modeling can greatly improve the efficiency and robustness of policy utilization of geometric information.
2024-03
Behavior Generation with Latent Actions
动机: Motivation: 针对高维连续动作空间下行为克隆（BC）效果差，以及扩散策略推理速度慢的痛点，寻求高效且通用的动作表示。To address the poor performance of Behavior Cloning (BC) under high-dimensional continuous action spaces and the slow inference speed of diffusion policies, seeking efficient and general action representations.
方法: Method: 提出 VQ-BeT，利用残差向量量化（Residual VQ）将连续动作离散化为 Token，结合 Transformer 进行预测。Proposed VQ-BeT, which utilizes Residual Vector Quantization (Residual VQ) to discretize continuous actions into tokens, combined with a Transformer for prediction.
推进: Advance: 实现了比扩散策略快 5 倍的推理速度，同时在仿真与真机上验证了离散化动作空间在复杂任务中的有效性与泛化能力。Achieved 5x faster inference speed compared to diffusion policies, while validating the effectiveness and generalization capability of discretized action spaces in complex tasks on both simulation and real robots.
2024-03
3D Diffusion Policy
动机: Motivation: 旨在提升扩散策略在灵巧手操作中的性能与安全性，同时解决现有 3D 方法推理慢且对长视界任务支持不足的问题。Aiming to improve the performance and safety of diffusion policies in dexterous hand manipulation, while addressing the slow inference of existing 3D methods and their insufficient support for long-horizon tasks.
方法: Method: 提出 DP3，将点云压缩为低维向量输入扩散策略，优化了 3D 表示与去噪过程，无需复杂 Transformer 堆叠。Proposed DP3, which compresses point clouds into low-dimensional vectors for input into diffusion policies, optimizing 3D representation and the denoising process without requiring complex Transformer stacking.
推进: Advance: 在 72 个仿真任务中相对提升 24.2%，且推理速度反超原版，同时显著降低了碰撞违规率，证明了高效 3D 表示的价值。Achieved a relative improvement of 24.2% across 72 simulation tasks, surpassed the original version in inference speed, and significantly reduced collision violation rates, demonstrating the value of efficient 3D representations.
2024-04
PLUTO: Pushing the Limit of Imitation Learning-based Planning for Autonomous Driving
动机: Motivation: 为了解决自动驾驶规划中纯模仿学习难以超越规则基线，且缺乏因果理解与长程规划能力的问题。To address the limitations of pure imitation learning in autonomous driving planning, which struggles to surpass rule-based baselines and lacks causal understanding and long-horizon planning capabilities.
方法: Method: 提出 PLUTO，采用混合架构，结合横向纵向 Query 融合与可微插值辅助损失，引入对比学习增强因果理解。Proposed PLUTO, adopting a hybrid architecture that combines lateral-longitudinal Query fusion and differentiable interpolation auxiliary loss, introducing contrastive learning to enhance causal understanding.
推进: Advance: 在 nuPlan 榜单上超越 PDM 等规则方法，证明了通过架构优化与辅助约束，模仿学习规划可达到甚至超越传统规则方法的性能。Surpassed rule-based methods like PDM on the nuPlan leaderboard, proving that imitation learning planning can reach or even exceed the performance of traditional rule-based methods through architectural optimization and auxiliary constraints.
2024-06
BAKU: An Efficient Transformer for Multi-Task Policy Learning
动机: Motivation: 针对多任务机器人学习中模型效率低、难以平衡数据效率与泛化性的问题，探索更高效的 Transformer 架构。To address low model efficiency and the difficulty in balancing data efficiency and generalization in multi-task robot learning, exploring more efficient Transformer architectures.
方法: Method: 提出 BAKU，通过组合 FiLM、Action Chunking 与独立 Action Head 构建多任务策略网络，适配 VQ-BeT 等模块。Proposed BAKU, constructing a multi-task policy network by combining FiLM, Action Chunking, and independent Action Heads, compatible with modules like VQ-BeT.
推进: Advance: 在 LIBERO 上实现 36% 的性能提升，证明了模块化工程优化在提升多任务数据效率与单技能执行精度上的有效性。Achieved a 36% performance improvement on LIBERO, demonstrating the effectiveness of modular engineering optimization in enhancing multi-task data efficiency and single-skill execution accuracy.

PerAct2: Benchmarking and Learning for Robotic Bimanual Manipulation Tasks
动机: Motivation: 针对双臂操作任务中现有基准测试匮乏及独立策略难以隐式协调的问题，旨在探索更通用的双操作行为克隆范式。Address the lack of existing benchmark tests in dual-arm manipulation tasks and the difficulty of independent policies in implicit coordination, aiming to explore a more general dual-arm behavior cloning paradigm.
方法: Method: 扩展 RLBench 构建包含 13 个任务的基准，提出单网络预测双臂动作的 PerAct2 架构，替代传统的独立 Agent 或 Leader-Follower 模式。Extend RLBench to build a benchmark containing 13 tasks, propose the PerAct2 architecture with a single network predicting dual-arm actions, replacing traditional independent Agent or Leader-Follower modes.
推进: Advance: 显著降低了显存占用并实现了隐式协调，但揭示了单纯行为克隆在处理复杂手递手等紧密耦合任务时的泛化瓶颈。Significantly reduce memory usage and achieve implicit coordination, but reveal generalization bottlenecks of pure behavior cloning when handling tightly coupled tasks like hand-to-hand transfers.

Multimodal Diffusion Transformer: Learning Versatile Behavior from Multimodal Goals
动机: Motivation: 旨在利用稀疏标注数据学习通用行为，解决传统方法依赖大量全标注数据且参数规模过大的问题。Aiming to learn general behaviors from sparse labeled data, addressing the issues of traditional methods relying on large amounts of fully labeled data and excessive parameter scales.
方法: Method: 提出 MDT，基于 Multimodal Diffusion Transformer，利用预训练 CLIP 和 Voltron 提取特征，实现从多模态目标到行为的映射。Proposed MDT, based on a Multimodal Diffusion Transformer, utilizing pre-trained CLIP and Voltron to extract features and achieve mapping from multi-modal targets to behaviors.
推进: Advance: 以不到 RoboFlamingo 十分之一的参数量在 CALVIN 上取得长 rollout 长度，证明了轻量化扩散模型在少样本场景下的潜力。Achieved long rollout lengths on CALVIN with less than one-tenth the parameters of RoboFlamingo, demonstrating the potential of lightweight diffusion models in few-shot scenarios.

Is Behavior Cloning All You Need? Understanding Horizon in Imitation Learning
动机: Motivation: 试图从理论层面打破“离线行为克隆必然存在二次样本复杂度依赖”的固有认知，探索 BC 的理论极限。Attempting to break the inherent cognitive belief that "offline behavior cloning inevitably suffers from quadratic sample complexity dependence" from a theoretical perspective, exploring the theoretical limits of BC.
方法: Method: 在可设定与参数共享假设下，证明使用对数损失的行为克隆可达到线性甚至无界依赖的样本复杂度。Under assumptions of set-ability and parameter sharing, proved that behavior cloning using log-loss can achieve linear or even unbounded dependence sample complexity.
推进: Advance: 提供了 BC 理论的新视角，揭示了在特定强假设下 BC 的优越性，但也指出了现实世界中假设失效导致的性能回退。Provides a new perspective on BC theory, revealing the superiority of BC under specific strong assumptions, while also pointing out performance degradation caused by the failure of assumptions in the real world.

方法: Method: 提出 RDT-1B，构建 1B 参数扩散基础模型，设计物理可解释的统一动作空间以对齐不同机器人数据。Proposed RDT-1B, constructing a 1B parameter diffusion foundation model and designing a physically interpretable unified action space to align data from different robots.
推进: Advance: 在 1M 数据预训练下实现了 SOTA 性能，证明了统一动作空间与大规模预训练在解决双臂操作泛化难题上的关键作用。Achieved SOTA performance under 1M data pre-training, demonstrating the critical role of unified action spaces and large-scale pre-training in solving the generalization challenges of dual-arm manipulation.

FlowPolicy: Enabling Fast and Robust 3D Flow-based Policy via Consistency Flow Matching for Robot Manipulation
动机: Motivation: 针对现有3D扩散策略推理速度慢、难以满足实时控制需求的问题，旨在探索更高效的生成机制。Addressing the slow inference speed of existing 3D diffusion policies and their difficulty in meeting real-time control requirements, this work aims to explore more efficient generation mechanisms.
方法: Method: 提出FlowPolicy，利用一致性流匹配（Consistency Flow Matching）替代传统扩散采样器，实现从3D点云到动作的快速映射。We propose FlowPolicy, utilizing consistency flow matching to replace traditional diffusion samplers, achieving rapid mapping from 3D point clouds to actions.
推进: Advance: 将推理时间从145ms大幅降低至20ms，在保持与DP3相当甚至更高成功率的同时，显著提升了策略的实时性与采样效率。We reduced inference time from 145ms to 20ms, significantly improving the real-time performance and sampling efficiency of the policy while maintaining success rates comparable to or higher than DP3.

推进: Advance: 实现了无需修改权重的轻量级适配，证明了在潜空间引导策略的可行性，为闭源大模型的快速部署提供了实用方案。Achieves lightweight adaptation without modifying weights, demonstrating the feasibility of guiding policies in latent space and providing a practical solution for rapid deployment of closed-source large models.

OopsieVerse: A Safety Benchmark with Damage-Aware Simulation for Robot Manipulation
动机: Motivation: 解决传统仿真中机器人仅追求任务成功而忽视物理安全（如损坏物体）的“暴力美学”问题，为模仿学习引入安全约束。Address the 'brute aesthetics' problem in traditional simulations where robots pursue task success while ignoring physical safety (e.g., damaging objects), introducing safety constraints for imitation learning.
方法: Method: 构建 OopsieVerse 仿真基准，集成 DamageSim 量化机械、热及流体伤害，提供跨平台的统一健康信号作为安全奖励。Construct the OopsieVerse simulation benchmark, integrating DamageSim to quantify mechanical, thermal, and fluid damage, providing a cross-platform unified health signal as a safety reward.
推进: Advance: 首次将“搞破坏式成功”与“安全成功”解耦，迫使 VLA 模型在模仿过程中学习避免物理损伤，填补了安全评估的空白。For the first time, decouple 'destructive success' from 'safe success', forcing VLA models to learn to avoid physical damage during imitation, filling the gap in safety evaluation.

World Action Models Enable Continual Imitation Learning with Recurrent Generative Replays
动机: Motivation: 应对具身智能在终身学习中面临的数据存储压力、隐私泄露风险及灾难性遗忘问题，寻求无需真实历史数据的复习机制。Address the data storage pressure, privacy leakage risks, and catastrophic forgetting problems faced by embodied intelligence in lifelong learning, seeking a review mechanism without real historical data.
方法: Method: 利用世界动作模型（WAM）的生成能力构建“记忆幻觉”，通过生成式回放（Recurrent Generative Replays）替代真实数据回放。Utilize the generative capabilities of World Action Models (WAM) to construct 'memory hallucinations', replacing real data replay with Recurrent Generative Replays.
推进: Advance: 证明了生成式回放能有效降低遗忘率并逼近真实回放效果，为无数据依赖的持续学习提供了新范式，尽管存在生成质量衰减问题。Demonstrate that generative replay effectively reduces forgetting rates and approaches the effects of real replay, providing a new paradigm for data-independent continuous learning, despite issues with generative quality degradation.

动机: Motivation: 解决 VLA 模型听懂指令但执行时物理可行性差、逻辑通顺但动作“爆炸”的痛点，探索推理时的行为修正机制。Address the pain point where VLA models understand instructions but have poor physical feasibility during execution, or logical coherence but 'explosive' actions, exploring behavior correction mechanisms during reasoning.

推进: Advance: 提供了一种不依赖重训的推理时行为干预手段，有效提升了物理交互的鲁棒性，但暴露了对 VLM 生成逻辑准确性的强依赖。Provide a reasoning-time behavior intervention method that does not rely on retraining, effectively improving the robustness of physical interaction, but exposing a strong dependency on the accuracy of VLM-generated logic.

DexVerse: A Modular Benchmark for Multi-Task, Multi-Embodiment Dexterous Manipulation
动机: Motivation: 针对现有灵巧操作基准过于单一且 SOTA 模型在复杂多任务场景下泛化能力不足的问题，旨在终结 Benchmark 内卷并揭示 VLA 在精细操作中的局限性。Aiming to address the issue that existing dexterous manipulation benchmarks are too singular and SOTA models lack generalization capabilities in complex multi-task scenarios, this work seeks to end the benchmark arms race and reveal the limitations of VLA in fine manipulation.

GraspGraphNet: Graph-Structured Multi-Embodiment Dexterous Grasp Generation
动机: Motivation: 旨在解决多机器人手抓取生成中的“巴别塔”难题，即如何直接输出可执行关节指令而无需事后优化或复杂的逆运动学求解。Aiming to solve the 'Tower of Babel' problem in multi-robot hand grasping generation, i.e., how to directly output executable joint commands without post-optimization or complex inverse kinematics solving.
方法: Method: 将机械手建模为图结构，利用 Flow Matching 生成速度场，直接输出拓扑感知的关节速度指令。Modeling the robotic hand as a graph structure, utilizing Flow Matching to generate velocity fields and directly outputting topology-aware joint velocity commands.
推进: Advance: 相比传统方法，实现了无需接触图优化的端到端生成，将推理速度提升至40ms，显著提高了实时控制效率。Compared to traditional methods, achieving end-to-end generation without contact graph optimization, increasing inference speed to 40ms and significantly improving real-time control efficiency.
2026-07
Learning Roller-Skating Motions of Humanoid Robots Based on Adversarial Motion Priors

动机: Motivation: 试图解决强化学习中状态压缩、模型学习与模仿学习之间的割裂问题，特别是从观察中进行高效模仿的理论基础。Attempts to resolve the fragmentation between state compression, model learning, and imitation learning in reinforcement learning, particularly the theoretical foundation for efficient imitation from observations.

世界模型与策略架构创新World Model and Strategy Architecture Innovation

本方向聚焦于世界模型（World Model）与策略架构的深度融合，旨在解决传统模仿学习中状态表征单一、规划与执行割裂的痛点。演进脉络从单纯引入多模态感知（如触觉）以增强状态完整性，转向构建双系统架构以解耦全局规划与局部微调，进而探索通过知识蒸馏将世界模型的物理先验迁移至轻量级策略。最新研究开始反思架构复杂性，强调时间结构而非视频内容本身的价值，推动领域从堆砌算力转向设计高效、可泛化的核心机制，实现从“预测未来”到“高效行动”的范式转变。This direction focuses on the deep integration of World Models and strategy architectures, aiming to solve the pain points of single state representation and the separation of planning and execution in traditional imitation learning. The evolutionary trajectory has shifted from simply introducing multi-modal perception (e.g., tactile) to enhance state completeness, to building dual-system architectures to decouple global planning from local fine-tuning, and further exploring the transfer of physical priors from world models to lightweight strategies via knowledge distillation. Latest research begins to reflect on architectural complexity, emphasizing the value of temporal structure over video content itself, driving the field from piling up computational power to designing efficient, generalizable core mechanisms, achieving a paradigm shift from 'predicting the future' to 'efficient action'.

Disentangling Spurious Correlations in Vision-Language-Action Models via Predicting Domain-Invariant Latent Lookahead
动机: Motivation: 现有VLA模型在仿真中表现优异但在真实世界失效，往往是因为学习了背景颜色等虚假相关性（Spurious Correlations）而非任务本质。Existing VLA models perform excellently in simulation but fail in the real world, often because they learn spurious correlations like background colors rather than the essence of the task.
方法: Method: 提出DILL方法，通过预测去除背景噪音的未来状态来解耦视觉特征，利用合成数据变换强制模型学习域不变表征。Proposes the DILL method, decoupling visual features by predicting future states with background noise removed, and forcing the model to learn domain-invariant representations through synthetic data transformations.

多模态数据生成与自动化标注Multimodal Data Generation and Automated Annotation

在多模态数据生成与自动化标注视角下，该方向正从依赖昂贵人工遥操作转向利用大模型与仿真技术实现低成本数据闭环。核心思路是通过Agent智能体将单张图像转化为高保真仿真数据，或利用物理信号与视觉语言模型协同解决复杂交互的标注难题。演进路径上，早期工作聚焦于单一模态的自动化，而近期研究（如ARSTAG）强调端到端的Agent驱动生成，试图填补Sim-to-Real鸿沟；同时（如Automatic Labelling）开始探索多模态融合（运动学+VLM）以提升标注在复杂双臂场景下的鲁棒性，尽管仍面临模型幻觉与泛化性挑战，但已显著降低了数据构建门槛。From the perspective of multimodal data generation and automated annotation, this direction is shifting from reliance on expensive manual teleoperation to utilizing large models and simulation technologies to achieve low-cost data loops. The core idea involves using Agent agents to convert single images into high-fidelity simulation data, or leveraging physical signals and vision-language models to collaboratively solve annotation challenges in complex interactions. In terms of evolution, early work focused on automation of single modalities, while recent research (e.g., ARSTAG) emphasizes end-to-end Agent-driven generation to bridge the Sim-to-Real gap; meanwhile (e.g., Automatic Labelling) began exploring multimodal fusion (kinematics + VLM) to improve annotation robustness in complex dual-arm scenarios, despite still facing challenges of model hallucination and generalization, yet significantly lowering the threshold for data construction.

ARSTAG: An Agentic Real2Sim2Real System for Task-Specific Robot Data Generation
动机: Motivation: 针对传统机器人数据获取高度依赖人工遥操作且成本高昂的问题，旨在利用大模型与Agent技术实现从单张图像到仿真数据的自动化生成。Addressing the problem that traditional robot data acquisition heavily relies on expensive manual teleoperation, it aims to utilize large models and Agent technologies to achieve automated generation from single images to simulation data.
方法: Method: 构建ARSTAG智能体系统，通过LLM规划与工具链调用，将单张RGB图像转化为可交互的仿真环境数据，并直接用于策略训练。Constructed the ARSTAG Agent system, which uses LLM planning and toolchain invocation to convert single RGB images into interactive simulation environment data, directly used for strategy training.
推进: Advance: 实现了无需人工干预的“单图变仿真”流程，在桌面任务中显著提升了数据生成效率，验证了Agent驱动生成在Sim-to-Real迁移中的可行性。Achieved a 'single image to simulation' workflow without human intervention, significantly improving data generation efficiency in desktop tasks and validating the feasibility of Agent-driven generation in Sim-to-Real transfer.
2026-09
Automatic Labelling for Bimanual Mobile Manipulation
动机: Motivation: 解决双臂移动操作任务中，纯视觉方法难以精准定位动作起止时间以及人工标注效率低下的痛点。Addressing the pain points in dual-arm mobile manipulation tasks where pure visual methods struggle to precisely locate action start and end times, and manual annotation efficiency is low.
方法: Method: 提出“物理运动检测+视觉语言模型”的协同框架，利用运动学信号锁定时间轴，引导VLM仅负责语义描述，实现自动化标注。Proposed a collaborative framework of 'physical motion detection + vision-language model', using kinematic signals to lock the timeline and guiding the VLM to focus solely on semantic description, achieving automated annotation.
推进: Advance: 通过解耦时间定位与语义理解，有效提升了复杂双臂交互场景下的标注一致性，为多模态数据标注提供了一种务实的工程化范式。By decoupling temporal localization and semantic understanding, it effectively improved annotation consistency in complex dual-arm interaction scenarios, providing a pragmatic engineering paradigm for multimodal data annotation.
